        $(function() {
            var generateColors = function (map) {
                var key,
                    colors = {};

                for (key in map.regions) {
                    colors[key] = ((has_videos.indexOf(key) !== -1) ? '#39B01F' : '#ccc');
                }

                return colors;
            }

            var franceMap = new jvm.Map({
                map: 'fr_regions_2016_mill',
                container: $('#fr-map'),
                backgroundColor: "transparent",
                zoomMax : 1,
                regionStyle: {
                    initial: {
                        fill: '#39B01F',
                    },
                    hover: {
                        fill: '#fd5fd6',
                    }

                },
                series: {
                    regions: [{
                        attribute: 'fill',
                    }]
                },
                onRegionClick: function(e, code) {
                    if (code in geo_urls && has_videos.indexOf(code) !== -1) {
                        window.location.replace(geo_urls[code]);
                    }

                    return true;
                },
                onRegionOver: function (event, code) {
                    if (has_videos.indexOf(code) === -1) {
                        event.preventDefault();
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            });

            var worldMap = new jvm.Map({
                map: 'world_merc',
                container: $('#world-map'),
                backgroundColor: "transparent",
                regionStyle: {
                    initial: {
                        fill: '#39B01F',
                    },
                    hover: {
                        fill: '#fd5fd6',
                    }
                },
                series: {
                    regions: [{
                        attribute: 'fill',
                    }]
                },
                onRegionClick: function(e, code) {
                    if (code == "FR") {
                        $("#mapfr").delay(100).fadeTo(400, 1);
                        $("#world-map-container").fadeOut(100);
                    }
                    else if (code in geo_urls && has_videos.indexOf(code) !== -1) {
                        window.location.replace(geo_urls[code]);
                    }

                    return true;
                },
                onRegionOver: function (event, code) {
                    if (has_videos.indexOf(code) === -1) {
                        event.preventDefault();
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            });


            franceMap.series.regions[0].setValues(generateColors(franceMap));
            worldMap.series.regions[0].setValues(generateColors(worldMap));
        });



        $('#btn-map-swap').on('click', function() {
            $( "#world-map-container" ).delay(100).fadeTo(400,1).resize();
            $( "#mapfr").fadeTo( 200, 0 );
        });

        $('#btn-map-back').on('click', function(){
            $( "#mapfr" ).delay(100).fadeTo( 400, 1 );
            $( "#world-map-container" ).fadeOut(100);
        });

        (function ($) {
          function isSafari() {
            var ua = navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase();
            return (ua.indexOf('safari') != -1) && (ua.indexOf('chrome') == -1);
          }

          function getParameterByName(name, url) {
            if (!url) url = window.location.href;
            name = name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, "\\$&");
            var regex = new RegExp("[?&]" + name + "(=([^&#]*)|&|#|$)"),
              results = regex.exec(url);
            if (!results) return null;
            if (!results[2]) return '';
            return decodeURIComponent(results[2].replace(/\+/g, " "));
          }

          var wCheck = "/payments/transaction/exists/?next=/";
          var wLoader = "/payments/loader/";
          var wFixSafari = "/payments/safari/fix/?next=https%3A//www.pornovoisines.com/%3Fdscl%3D1&w=";
          var redirectedUrl = getParameterByName('w');

          var wOpen = function (uri, safariFixApplied) {
            if (!uri) {
              uri = $("[data-default-purchase-url]").data("wopen");
            }

            if (isSafari() && !safariFixApplied) {
              window.location.href = wFixSafari + encodeURIComponent(uri);
            } else {
              wallet.open(uri, wLoader, wCheck);
            }

          };

          $(document).ready(function () {

            $('body').on('click', '[data-wopen]', function (event) {
              event.preventDefault();
              wOpen($(this).data("wopen"), false);
            });

            if (redirectedUrl) {
              wOpen(redirectedUrl, true);
            }
          });


          $(document).ready(function () {
            $('[data-search] [data-search-selector]').change(function () {
              var selection = $(this).val();
              $('[data-search-type][data-search-type!=' + selection + ']').hide();
              $('[data-search-type=' + selection + ']').show();
            });

            $('[data-search]').submit(function (event) {
              event.preventDefault();
              var $selector = $(this).find('[data-search-type]:visible');
              if ($selector.data('field-type') === 'select') {
                document.location.href = $selector.find('select').val();
              } else {
                if ($selector.val().trim().length === 0) {
                  return;
                }
                document.location.href = $selector.data('search-url').replace('QUERY', encodeURI($selector.val()));
              }
            });
          });
        })(jQuery);
    </script>
